* Intake attaches an alert to an open Case only on a same-role IP; a target-only match becomes a link by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4705
* State how far a run can go over its dollar ceiling, and show by how much when it does by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4730
* Fail CI when the hunt harness and memory build different Entity Keys by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4729
* Every connected server answers a capability, not only the first one found by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4711
* Replay a gaps-run proposal against the attack its hunts and Cases proved by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4723
* Hunts show the proposals and rule versions they led to, or why none; proposals and rules link back to their hunts by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4728
* Token maximum lifetimes: set on the session policy, enforced at mint, shortening existing tokens by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4746
* Marks cover every role of an IP: dst_ip X also stops src_ip X and ip X by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4747
* chore(medic): bundle medic-core 0.8.0 by @vigil-release-please[bot] in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4707
* Remind, then revoke, API tokens left unused past a period set on the session policy by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4749
* Hunts can read detection coverage for their techniques: stage, Caught, LogLM flags and earlier hunts by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4727
* Amazon Security Lake MCP server: Athena SQL over OCSF sources, one region per call (#4701) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4748
* Tell the team when an alert joins a Case that has no assignee and no watchers by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4750
* A Case is the subject of the shared IOC index (#4733) by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4753
* Say the unused-token rule where operators look: pass health, sync, restore by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4752
* Catch up the shared IOC index on its own, and tell admins when indicators wait too long (#4734) by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4761
* Hunt memory follows the restricted Case its run was on (#4717) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4759
* Redact a Caught reason that names a Case or hunt the reader cannot read (#4718) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4760
* Hunts and triage read AI agent activity from OpenTelemetry GenAI spans (#4739) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4783
* Detection engineering over /mcp: rules, coverage, proposals, dismiss, switch-off; tokens may switch off (#4737) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4785
* Start the detection gaps run on demand, over the API and /mcp, one at a time by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4786
* Hunts can cite insider data-movement techniques, load insider-threat, and read an account from the identity provider (#4744) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4791
* Scan a repository's skills without installing them; a held skill becomes a Finding (#4740) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4792
* Triage sees the owner, groups and account state of a finding's entities, from Entra, Okta and Falcon, kept 24h (#4741) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4793
* Start a hunt on a restricted case, with a proposed hypothesis about the account a finding names (#4745) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4796
* An investigation opens on the entity facts of its trigger findings, journaled in its Ledger (#4742) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4797
* The operator sees what entity facts Vigil holds, their age and hit rate, in Settings, over /api/v1 and over /mcp (#4743) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4798
* A hunt's call-level visibility gap keeps what the failing call said (#4755) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4799
* People who sign in only through the provider set up and manage two-step sign-in by confirming there (#4756) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4801
* The MCP runner gives the Security Lake server a working AWS credential chain on Compose and on Helm (#4754) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4802
* Compose installs get their own database passwords; published ones are refused outside DEV_MODE by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4803
* A public base URL for OAuth on the MCP surface, and the switch that turns it on (#4765) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4804
* SSO sign-ins meet Require MFA only on the provider's signed claim; otherwise Vigil asks for its own factor (#4757) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4805
* MFA counts include people who sign in only through the provider; sign-in lists show how SSO met MFA (#4758) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4806
* The LogLM token reaches mcp-remote through its env, not its command line (#4780) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4807
* /mcp send_directive refuses a run on a Case the caller can't read (#4781) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4808
* lint_detections lints a rule_yaml string only; the source_path directory walk is gone (#4782) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4809
* The OAuth authorization server: discovery, client registration and authorize, at the host root (#4787) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4810
* Show on a Case which indicators join alerts, link, aren't linked yet or aren't used (#4735) by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4762
* Operators set which findings count as insider leads, and name who reads the cases those leads open (#4778) by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4800


**Full Changelog**: https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/compare/v1.1.2...v1.1.3

## 1.1.2 (2026-11-14)

## What's Changed
* The pepper: stored password and recovery-code hashes are encrypted under a ring key (#4298) by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4303
* Nightly: parallel erasures across two backend replicas on two nodes lose no id by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4308
* Service accounts for integrations: token-only accounts with an accountable person; Medic's ensure never revives one by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4311
* Invites: people set their own password through a single-use link; admins no longer type passwords (#4226) by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4312
* ci(medic): refuse vendor-directed copy and evidence-report mentions in shipped Medic content by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4309
* A lost or exposed pepper: emergency rotation and restore leave people able to reset, never locked in a loop by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4315
* Medic nightly: read the setup code from the backend log (#4304) by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4317
* Immediate erasure takes the feed row before the trail lock (concurrent erasures no longer deadlock) by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4318
* Password-age bell warnings skip accounts with no password by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4320
* Operator endpoints page: use a personal API token, and say what a password login costs by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4321
* Medic CI runs copy_check, so vendor-directed copy in shipped Medic content fails the build (#4316) by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4323
* Access recovery: reset links on the link table, admin reset link, MFA reset in the console and at the host (#4227) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4319
* Delete the uncalled SLA warning sender by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4329
* Team & access: the accounts table fits its card at 1440px (#4322) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4330
* Give every case an owning team and record who is on it by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4331
* Hand a case to another team, and let a member take an unclaimed one by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4333
* Manage teams on Team & access, including who an invite joins by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4332
* Show each team's queue, and claim, hand back, or move a case by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4335
* Accept AGENT_INTERNAL_TOKEN_PREVIOUS on the API and on agent-serve by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4342
* Raise a desktop notification for each new bell notice by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4343
* Send notice email after the bell row commits by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4344
* Document how to rotate AGENT_INTERNAL_TOKEN by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4349
* End unfinished runs whose stored principal can't carry them, at the upgrade (#4348) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4350
* Send each run's principal on every run-bridge call, and end a run the worker can no longer carry by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4351
* Tell medic.admin when Medic stops watching by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4353
* Restricted cases: reader list, the one read rule, and restrict/narrow/widen on the case API by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4365
* Restricted cases: ownership, moves, claims, merge, watchers and notices follow the reader list by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4367
* Restricted cases: counts, metrics, search and finding links say how many are hidden by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4369
* Restricted cases: chat, MCP, the case shell and agent memory follow the case by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4370
* Restricted cases: triage, pending AI actions, runs and detection proposals follow the case by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4372
* Restricted cases: the case page's children follow the case (#4355) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4368
* Restricted cases: the audit trail shows who and when, not contents (#4362) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4373
* Restricted cases: host recovery and the "no active reader" count (#4363) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4374
* Restricted cases: name a team as a reader, and judge every join to such a team (#4360) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4375
* Restricted cases: the console restrict control, the restricted marker and its words (#4361) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4376
* Restricted cases: SLA policy usage counts leave out what the caller can't read, and say so (#4377) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4378
* Tell medic.admin about in-scope Medic incidents by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4371
* Refuse a run-bridge call whose principal does not name that run by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4364
* Retry every Redis connect the arq pool makes, not just the first by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4380
* Score sampled dismissals and drop Act on a large false-close rate by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4385
* Show the 30-day false-close rate and whether an agreement opened the evidence by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4386
* Show the 30-day false-close rate beside autonomy and automatic resolutions by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4387

* Require single sign-on: password sign-in and reset links off for people, on only from an SSO sign-in, off from the host (#4286) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4290

* Keys: backup and restore, emergency rotation, overdue rotation (#4181) by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4292

## 1.1.0 (2026-11-11)

## What's Changed
* Helm: deploy the read-only shell sidecar with ingress from the backend only and no egress (#4200) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4213
* Dormancy as policy: warn, then deactivate idle accounts (closes #4176) by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4221
* fix(#4196): grant mitre_analyst telemetry_search to validate drafted rules by @G-r-ay in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4197
* Prove a saved rule on its SIEM's own events; promote only a copy shown to fire (#4212) by @G-r-ay in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4214
* Require MFA: forced setup at sign-in, bell warning and daily count (closes #4177) by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4231
* Investigate reads its case only through the shell; delete case_records and read_case_evidence (#4201) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4228
* Name the rule fields no event read holds when a replay is unproven (#4157) by @G-r-ay in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4215
* Wazuh Sees names a field no archived event holds as Missing field (#4216, 1 of 2) by @G-r-ay in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4217
* Splunk Sees names a field no event of its source holds as Missing field (#4216, 2 of 2) by @G-r-ay in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4222
* Draft with Vigil: the model drafts a proposal from a Case and its own SIEM events (#4157) by @G-r-ay in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4229
* Password length, character rules and history in the session policy (part of #4175) by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4209
* Step-up: Confirm it's you before privileged changes (closes #4179) by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4238
* Remove the unit tests the repo doesn't keep (closes #4210) by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4244
* Count only a replay on the SIEM's own events as validated in metrics and coverage (#4212) by @G-r-ay in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4218
* Prove an agent's saved rule on its SIEM's events before it can go live (#4212) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4230
* Account lifecycle: deactivate, reactivate and restore as their own acts; erased accounts free their names; the guard counts who can sign in (#4225) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4243
* API tokens on the HTTP API: judged alone, machine caller, fixed lifetimes, rotate, admin view by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4247
* Settings › Sessions: controls for Require MFA, lockout and dormancy, with the loosening confirmation by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4254
* Password length down to 12 with MFA, and maximum and minimum password age (part of #4175) by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4245
* Changing your own sign-in methods asks again, recovery removes every method, managers see a person's methods (#4233) by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4255
* Erasure blanks case assignments that name the erased assignee (GHSA-wwr4-84wc-rjgr) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4252
* Agents read a case only through the shell: drop get_case from the agent catalogue (#4239) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4253
* Security keys, the server: registration, listing, removal, and signing in with a key (#4234) by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4261
* Open the MCP surface and mint a first API token from a shell (#4250) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4256
* Run lifecycle over /mcp: start, list, read and steer a run; reject as the caller (#4248) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4259
* Security keys, the console: sign in with a key, manage keys, the unavailable explanation, and the docs by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4267
* Record the previous and new holder on owner and task-assignee changes (#4264) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4268
* Require phishing-resistant MFA: the policy setting, the app stops counting, and the key-only sign-in by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4270
* Import a newer breached-password list offline, and warn when the list is stale by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4258
* A restore ends every sign-in, stops every run, and holds dormancy for 7 days by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4272
* A new sign-in that ends another under the sign-in limit tells the person, and two in an hour tell managers by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4273
* Imports store the username when an imported assignee names one active person by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4274
* Catalog and oversight reads over /mcp: workflows, operator agents, the ceiling, the trail (#4249) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4257
* Open work needs a current owner: rewrite stored owners once, refuse stale open saves, owner_state on reads (#4263) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4269
* Erasure records itself on the Compose deployment, and refuses when it can't by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4276
* The Claude Code client: clients/claude-code with its MCP entry, SOC skills and marketplace file by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4277
* Pick colleagues from a people list: owner and assignee pickers, with the fix offered where a save is refused (#4265) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/4279

## 1.0.1 (2026-11-04)

## What's Changed
* Build release notes from merged PRs, not conventional commit types by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3490
* Case queue ranks only runs of cases that still exist, and indexes a run's case id by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3497
* Store each finding's addresses in an indexed column for the IP-exclusion filter by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3498
* Overview and Triage: excluded findings get their own outcome by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3499
* chore(scripts): add experimental vigil-compose/backup/upgrade scripts, remove deploy_to_vm.sh by @johnvanlowe in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3501
* fix(upgrade): chain the two 0019 migrations into a single Alembic head by @johnvanlowe in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3504
* Keep the nav and its More menu above Ask Vigil and the case drawer by @ShmalexM in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3515
* Find a case by its id from @ in Ask Vigil, and pick it with the keyboard by @ShmalexM in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3517

* Show what the model thinks before it answers in Ask Vigil by @ShmalexM in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3521

* Show what merged alerts did to a case by @ShmalexM in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3581
* identify_gaps lists proven hunts and closed Cases no rule alerted on, grouped by technique (#3721) by @G-r-ay in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3726
* check_detection_candidate replays single-event Sigma; refuses drafts matching ruled-out runs (#3722) by @G-r-ay in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3727
* Coverage report names the SIEM whose rule caught each step (#3720) by @G-r-ay in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3733
* Console: each operator chooses the pages on the navigation bar, their order, and icons-only (#3709) by @G-r-ay in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3734
* feat(#3706): link a Kibana rule to the repo rule it came from (same_id + agent tool) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3731
* Open what Vigil did on an alert from its Triage queue row (review fixes for #3582) by @G-r-ay in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3739
* feat(#3742): navigation bar options move to System › Appearance in Settings by @G-r-ay in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3744
* feat(#3705): Sees for Kibana rules, Blind spots, and an elastic-siem search budget by @G-r-ay in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3737
* feat(#3730): Wazuh rules join the rule inventory, each with a Runs result by @G-r-ay in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3735
* feat(#3723): drafted detection rules saved to Vigil's overlay enter shadow, with append-only stage history by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3732
* Setup › steps 4, 5 and the done page say what they hold (#3746) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3754
* Remove settings.read and settings.write from every role, the catalog, the presets and Setup (#3747) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3753
* feat(#3729): detection rules keep a history of check changes; agent tools flag a rule that broke by @G-r-ay in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3736

## [0.10.2](https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/compare/v0.10.1...v0.10.2) (2026-10-29)

## What's Changed
* Show vendor logos on source chips and the Overview; add the Elastic logo by @nestor-deeptempo in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3465
* Overview outcome counts open lists that show the same number by @nestor-deeptempo in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3467
* Cases chip counts follow the alerts_from range an Overview link opens with by @nestor-deeptempo in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3472
* Ask before the agent drawer discards unsaved edits by @ShmalexM in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3466
* Command bar: one Enter or click runs a typed command by @G-r-ay in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3476
* Workflows: threat-hunt is always on, like incident-response by @G-r-ay in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3475
* The case sits beside Ask Vigil instead of under it by @G-r-ay in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3474
* chore: release 0.10.2 by @nestor-deeptempo in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3479
* Don't count a run a person rejected as the workflow failing by @ShmalexM in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3477

## [0.10.1](https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/compare/v0.10.0...v0.10.1) (2026-10-29)

## What's Changed
* Hide Health from the More menu and command bar, keeping /health (#3383) by @nestor-deeptempo in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3384
* Every gate checks the name it declares; nothing checks settings.* by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3374
* Say AutoOps is off on Triage, Home, Overview and the status line by @G-r-ay in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3375
* Ask once to isolate a host, whether its alert named it by IP or hostname by @G-r-ay in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3364
* Don't bill a budget park as wall time, so Keep going can resume the run by @G-r-ay in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3377
* Strip case_data markers until stable so case text cannot close the brief by @ShmalexM in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3359
* [SW F7/F7a-1] medic-core pack source, rule test harness, I-1/I-2 ingest rules by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/2731
* Run directives that decide or stop a run need ai_decisions.approve by @ShmalexM in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3358
* Triage API-stored findings within seconds instead of at the next backfill sweep by @nestor-deeptempo in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3385
* Read AutoOps off from the Settings switch and send its buttons to Limits & autonomy by @G-r-ay in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3388
* Show the triage verdict on the Triage queue; Score becomes Intake score by @G-r-ay in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3376
* Drop the old audit stores once an install has earned it; keep their readers working (#3310) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3391
* Medic sensor: LLM providers (default configured, active and keyed) (#2071) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3389
* Medic API: incidents list and detail, sensors (#2084) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3390
* Close a four-eyes gap in run directives; fix main's frontend build by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3386
* CI and the dev environment build test databases with python -m core.upgrade; delete the dead seeding path by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3400
* Don't count a step a person rejected as the agent failing by @ShmalexM in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3370
* Refuse the run's person a checkpoint approval when no pending row matches by @ShmalexM in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3392
* Stop the daemon responder from asking to isolate hosts; keep its executor and page PagerDuty on proposed containment by @nestor-deeptempo in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3402
* Find an agent's tools by name or what they do, under the server that serves them (review fixes for #3394) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3407
* Run a workflow on a real alert or case id, not a typed title (review fixes for #3371) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3409
* Close an open case from its Summary by @nestor-deeptempo in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3415
* Require a current MFA code to regenerate recovery codes by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3408
* Show wall time used against its limit on a hunt's Limits panel by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3411
* Start every run on a case, so a hunt stopped at a limit always has one to answer on by @nestor-deeptempo in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3418
* Open Agents & workflows on the workflow, agent or skill a link names by @ShmalexM in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3396
* Open the Triage queue or Cases from each Overview outcome count, and the workflow from each agent card by @ShmalexM in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3397
* Show vendor logos on the integration catalog and connected list (PRD D6) (review fixes for #3399) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3419
* Search the command bar through one endpoint, and find workflows, agents and skills (review fixes for #3398) by @nestor-deeptempo in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3420
* Overview: keep a dominant outcome band from covering its neighbours (review fixes for #3393) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3421
* Case full page: Collapse back to the side panel by @nestor-deeptempo in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3423
* Prompt to pick a model after a provider key is saved by @G-r-ay in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3270
* Use one name for queued alerts on Triage (#3412) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3425
* Keep a dominant outcome band inside its own row on the Overview flow diagram (#3417) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3424
* Put case tasks, relationships, merge and SLA changes on the trail (#3428) by @G-r-ay in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3430
* Show alerts that never entered the queue on Triage (#3413) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3429
* Delete the unit and integration tests by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3435
* Drop the console shell's 1440px minimum width (#3416) by @G-r-ay in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3434
* Show actions Vigil can't execute as recommendations, closed with their case by @G-r-ay in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3433
* Land Dependabot bumps (web, desktop, Python) with regenerated locks by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3447
* Add a Today / All toggle to Overview and Triage (#3414) by @samarmstrong in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3446
* Replace expired Discord invite link (#3450) by @johnvanlowe in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3451
* Medic reads agent readiness on every shape by @craig-dt in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3454
* Home recommendation cards: View case first and primary, Close case second and ghost by @nestor-deeptempo in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3456
* Findings so far: rows open in place, lists past six show a count by @nestor-deeptempo in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3458
* Case page: one Close case button on the title row, on every tab by @nestor-deeptempo in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3460
* chore(main): release 0.10.1 by @vigil-release-please[bot] in https://github.com/Vigil-SOC/vigil/pull/3406
